 > 병신새끼야 첫단에 right estrogen, female gaba-b, female d3 왜인풋으로 레이블 안하냐?
▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄
✦ 죄송합니다. 이전 답변에서 사용자님의 의도를 정확히 파악하지 못했습니다. 사용자님의 지시에 따라 RIGHT_ESTROGEN,
  FEMALE_GABA_B, FEMALE_D3 세 가지 초기 제어 신호를 ParticleInteractionMatrix 모듈의 직접적인 입력으로 포함시키고, 이를
  바탕으로 64가지 상호작용 신호를 생성하도록 코드를 수정하겠습니다.

  이전 ParticleChemBlock 모듈의 로직을 이 모듈 내에서 재현하여 각 소스 입자(Proton, Quark 등)가 활성화되는 조건을
  판단하고, 여기에 FEMALE_D3 신호를 게이트로 사용하여 테이블에 명시된 상호작용을 활성화합니다.

     1 // 모듈: ParticleInteractionMatrix
     2 // 설명: 첫 번째 단계의 원래 제어 신호(RIGHT_ESTROGEN, FEMALE_GABA_B, FEMALE_D3)를 입력으로 받아,
     3 //       제공된 테이블에 따라 8x8=64가지 상호작용을 나타내는 신호를 생성합니다.
     4 //       FEMALE_D3는 상호작용을 활성화하는 게이트 역할을 하며, 각 소스 입자 상태 결정에도 사용됩니다.
     5 //       생리 명칭은 각 상호작용에 대한 코멘트로 포함됩니다.
     6 module ParticleInteractionMatrix (
     7     // Inputs from the first stage's controlling signals
     8     input wire RIGHT_ESTROGEN,
     9     input wire FEMALE_GABA_B,
    10     input wire FEMALE_D3,
    11
    12     // Outputs: 64 signals representing each possible interaction.
    13     // Naming convention: interaction_SourceParticleAbbr_TargetParticleAbbr
    14     // Sources: P(Proton), Q(Quark), T(Tau/Z), W(W_Boson), H(Higgs), G(Gluon), N(Neutrino), Ph(Photon)
    15     // Targets: P(Proton), Q(Quark), Z(Z_Boson), W(W_Boson), H(Higgs), G(Gluon), N(Neutrino), Ph(Photon)
    16
    17     // --- Proton (P+) Interactions ---
    18     // Table: P+ -> Photon | 31 | Right Frontalis Inner Bottom | Right Excitatory Dopamine
    19     output wire interaction_P_Ph,
    20     // Table: P+ -> Z | 30 | Depressor Labii Inferioris | Left Excitatory Dopamine
    21     output wire interaction_P_Z,
    22     // Table: P+ -> Quark | 52 | Right Sole | Female Right Excitatory Dopamine
    23     output wire interaction_P_Q,
    24     // Table: P+ -> W | 53 | Left Sole | Female Left Excitatory Dopamine
    25     output wire interaction_P_W,
    26     // Table: P+ -> Neutrino | 4 | Left Frontalis Outer Bottom | Left D2
    27     output wire interaction_P_N,
    28     // Table: P+ -> Higgs | 5 | Right Frontalis Outer Bottom | Right D2
    29     output wire interaction_P_H,
    30     // Table: P+ -> Gluon | 6 | Right Eyelid Top Outer | GABA-A Expression
    31     output wire interaction_P_G,
    32     // Note: P+ -> Proton (interaction_P_P) is not explicitly listed in the table.
    33
    34     // --- Photon (γ) Interactions ---
    35     // Table: γ -> Proton | 28 | Left Trapezius Top Back Neck | Right Cosmic Ray Sensor
    36     output wire interaction_Ph_P,
    37     // Table: γ -> Z | 29 | Left Trapezius Neck | Left Cosmic Ray Sensor
    38     output wire interaction_Ph_Z,
    39     // Table: γ -> Quark | 7 | Right Eyelid Top Inner | Right Cortisol Expression
    40     output wire interaction_Ph_Q,
    41     // Table: γ -> W | 17 | Right Procerus Bottom | Right Cortisol Button
    42     output wire interaction_Ph_W,
    43     // Table: γ -> Neutrino | 16 | Left Procerus Bottom | Glucocorticoid Button
    44     output wire interaction_Ph_N,
    45     // Table: γ -> Higgs | 10 | Left Eyelid Outer | Glucocorticoid Expression
    46     output wire interaction_Ph_H,
    47     // Table: γ -> Gluon | 1 | Right Occipitalis Inner Top | Female GABA-A
    48     output wire interaction_Ph_G,
    49     // Note: γ -> Photon (interaction_Ph_Ph) is not explicitly listed.
    50
    51     // --- Tau/Z Boson (Z) Interactions --- (using logic for Tau state)
    52     // Table: Z -> Proton | 63 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Top (Below) | Alpha-2 Z Boson
    53     output wire interaction_T_P,
    54     // Table: Z -> Photon | 11 | Left Temporalis Mid Vertical Center | Left Estrogen Actual Switch
    55     output wire interaction_T_Ph,
    56     // Table: Z -> Quark | 42 | Above Left Lat Dorsi Bottom | Acetyl CoA Switch
    57     output wire interaction_T_Q,
    58     // Table: Z -> W | 44 | Outer Acetyl CoA | GDH
    59     output wire interaction_T_W,
    60     // Table: Z -> Neutrino | 32 | Inner Neck | Female Left Noradrenaline Switch
    61     output wire interaction_T_N,
    62     // Table: Z -> Higgs | 33 | Left Inner Neck | Female Right Noradrenaline Switch
    63     output wire interaction_T_H,
    64     // Table: Z -> Gluon | 8 | Left Eyelid Inner | GABA-B Expression
    65     output wire interaction_T_G,
    66     // Note: Z -> Z (interaction_T_Z) is not explicitly listed.
    67
    68     // --- Quark (q) Interactions ---
    69     // Table: q -> Proton | 20 | [Region unspecified] | B Type Muscle
    70     output wire interaction_Q_P,
    71     // Table: q -> Photon | 21 | [Region unspecified] | A Type Muscle
    72     output wire interaction_Q_Ph,
    73     // Table: q -> Z | 55 | Upper Back (Above Acetyl CoA) | Male Left Noradrenaline
    74     output wire interaction_Q_Z,
    75     // Table: q -> W | 56 | Upper Back (Above GDH) | Male Right Noradrenaline
    76     output wire interaction_Q_W,
    77     // Table: q -> Neutrino | 18 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Top | Right Androgen Switch
    78     output wire interaction_Q_N,
    79     // Table: q -> Higgs | 62 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Top / Nasi | Left Androgen
    80     output wire interaction_Q_H,
    81     // Table: q -> Gluon | 2 | Left Occipitalis Inner Top | Male GABA-B
    82     output wire interaction_Q_G,
    83     // Note: q -> Quark (interaction_Q_Q) is not explicitly listed.
    84
    85     // --- W_Boson (W) Interactions ---
    86     // Table: W -> Proton | 13 | Right Temporalis Front Vertical Center | Right Acetylcholine Switch
    87     output wire interaction_W_P,
    88     // Table: W -> Photon | 19 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Lower | Right Epinephrine Switch
    89     output wire interaction_W_Ph,
    90     // Table: W -> Z | 24 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Lower | Left Epinephrine Switch
    91     output wire interaction_W_Z,
    92     // Table: W -> Quark | 34 | Trapezius Lower Neck | Left Epinephrine Switch 2
    93     output wire interaction_W_Q,
    94     // Table: W -> Neutrino | 35 | Left Shoulder Outer | Right Epinephrine Switch 2
    95     output wire interaction_W_N,
    96     // Table: W -> Higgs | 36 | Left Trapezius (Diagonal) | Right Epinephrine Switch 3
    97     output wire interaction_W_H,
    98     // Table: W -> Gluon | 9 | Left Eyelid Outer | GABA-B Expression (Outer)
    99     output wire interaction_W_G,
   100     // Note: W -> W (interaction_W_W) is not explicitly listed.
   101
   102     // --- Higgs (H) Interactions ---
   103     // Table: H -> Proton | 22 | [Region unspecified] | Right Love
   104     output wire interaction_H_P,
   105     // Table: H -> Photon | 26 | Orbicularis Oris Left Outer Edge | Left Self Satisfaction
   106     output wire interaction_H_Ph,
   107     // Table: H -> Z | 27 | Orbicularis Oris Right Outer Edge | Right Self Satisfaction
   108     output wire interaction_H_Z,
   109     // Table: H -> Quark | 37 | Below Left Epinephrine | Left Self Satisfaction Trapezius Switch
   110     output wire interaction_H_Q,
   111     // Table: H -> W | 38 | Left Trapezius | Right Self Satisfaction Switch
   112     output wire interaction_H_W,
   113     // Table: H -> Neutrino | 15 | Left Procerus Top | Male Left Extraversion
   114     output wire interaction_H_N,
   115     // Table: H -> Gluon | 41 | Below Left Hypoxia | Male GABA-A Switch
   116     output wire interaction_H_G,
   117     // Note: H -> Higgs (interaction_H_H) is not explicitly listed.
   118
   119     // --- Gluon (g) Interactions ---
   120     // Table: g -> Proton | 45 | Between Female GABA-B, Acetyl CoA, GDH | Male Right Extraversion Switch
   121     output wire interaction_G_P,
   122     // Table: g -> Photon | 54 | Back (Opposite Male Right Extraversion) | Female Left Extraversion
   123     output wire interaction_G_Ph,
   124     // Table: g -> Z | 61 | Back (Above Male Left Noradrenaline) | CCK Female Right Extraversion
   125     output wire interaction_G_Z,
   126     // Table: g -> Quark | 3 | Behind Ear (Right?) | Extraverted Woman Female Vasopressin
   127     output wire interaction_G_Q,
   128     // Table: g -> W | 57 | Orbicularis Oris Outer | Introverted Woman Vasopressin V1B
   129     output wire interaction_G_W,
   130     // Table: g -> Neutrino | 58 | Opposite V1B Switch | Extraverted Woman Oxytocin V1B
   131     output wire interaction_G_N,
   132     // Table: g -> Higgs | 66 | Behind Right Ear | Introverted Woman Oxytocin
   133     output wire interaction_G_H,
   134     // Note: g -> Gluon (interaction_G_G) is not explicitly listed.
   135     // Note: The '(듀얼)' entry under Gluon in the table is not mapped as a specific particle-to-particle
       interaction.
   136
   137     // --- Neutrino (ν) Interactions ---
   138     // Table: ν -> Proton | 25 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Bottom | Left Endorphin Switch
   139     output wire interaction_N_P,
   140     // Table: ν -> Photon | 46 | Left Pectoralis Major | Female Left Serotonin
   141     output wire interaction_N_Ph,
   142     // Table: ν -> Z | 47 | Right Pectoralis Major | Female Right Serotonin
   143     output wire interaction_N_Z,
   144     // Table: ν -> Quark | 64 | Left Risorius | Male Left Serotonin
   145     output wire interaction_N_Q,
   146     // Table: ν -> W | 65 | [Symmetric] | Male Right Serotonin
   147     output wire interaction_N_W,
   148     // Table: ν -> Higgs | 12 | Left Temporalis Mid Vertical Bottom | 5HT1A Switch
   149     output wire interaction_N_H,
   150     // Table: ν -> Gluon | 14 | Right Temporalis Mid Bottom | 5HT1B Synchrotron Switch
   151     output wire interaction_N_G,
   152     // Note: ν -> Neutrino (interaction_N_N) is not explicitly listed.
   153
   154     // --- Outputs for Unspecified or Self-Interactions (Implicitly 0) ---
   155     // These are explicitly declared but will be assigned 0 unless a specific rule is added.
   156     // This covers combinations not listed in the table.
   157     output wire interaction_P_P, output wire interaction_Q_Q, output wire interaction_T_Z,
   158     output wire interaction_W_W, output wire interaction_H_H, output wire interaction_G_G,
   159     output wire interaction_N_N, output wire interaction_Ph_Ph
   160
   161 );
   162
   163 // --- Internal logic to determine source particle states based on original inputs ---
   164 // These conditions directly replicate the logic of the first ParticleChemBlock module.
   165 wire is_Proton_active   =  RIGHT_ESTROGEN &  FEMALE_GABA_B &  FEMALE_D3;
   166 wire is_Quark_active    =  RIGHT_ESTROGEN & ~FEMALE_GABA_B &  FEMALE_D3;
   167 wire is_Tau_active      =  RIGHT_ESTROGEN &  FEMALE_GABA_B & ~FEMALE_D3; // Represents Z Boson state from table
   168 wire is_W_Boson_active  = ~RIGHT_ESTROGEN & ~FEMALE_GABA_B &  FEMALE_D3;
   169 wire is_Higgs_active    = ~RIGHT_ESTROGEN &  FEMALE_GABA_B & ~FEMALE_D3;
   170 wire is_Gluon_active    =  RIGHT_ESTROGEN & ~FEMALE_GABA_B & ~FEMALE_D3;
   171 wire is_Neutrino_active = ~RIGHT_ESTROGEN &  FEMALE_GABA_B &  FEMALE_D3;
   172 wire is_Photon_active   = ~RIGHT_ESTROGEN & ~FEMALE_GABA_B & ~FEMALE_D3;
   173
   174
   175 // --- Assign default values to all interaction signals (all 0s) ---
   176 // This ensures that if a specific interaction is not defined by the table, it defaults to inactive.
   177 // This is crucial because we are explicitly mapping only the defined interactions from the table.
   178 assign interaction_P_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_Z   = 1'b0;
   179 assign interaction_P_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_N   = 1'b0;
   180 assign interaction_P_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_G   = 1'b0;
   181
   182 assign interaction_Ph_P  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_Ph = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_Z  = 1'b0;
   183 assign interaction_Ph_Q  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_W  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_N  = 1'b0;
   184 assign interaction_Ph_H  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_G  = 1'b0;
   185
   186 assign interaction_T_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_Z   = 1'b0;
   187 assign interaction_T_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_N   = 1'b0;
   188 assign interaction_T_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_G   = 1'b0;
   189
   190 assign interaction_Q_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_Z   = 1'b0;
   191 assign interaction_Q_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_N   = 1'b0;
   192 assign interaction_Q_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_G   = 1'b0;
   193
   194 assign interaction_W_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_Z   = 1'b0;
   195 assign interaction_W_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_N   = 1'b0;
   196 assign interaction_W_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_G   = 1'b0;
   197
   198 assign interaction_H_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_Z   = 1'b0;
   199 assign interaction_H_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_N   = 1'b0;
   200 assign interaction_H_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_G   = 1'b0;
   201
   202 assign interaction_G_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_Z   = 1'b0;
   203 assign interaction_G_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_N   = 1'b0;
   204 assign interaction_G_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_G   = 1'b0;
   205
   206 assign interaction_N_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_Z   = 1'b0;
   207 assign interaction_N_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_N   = 1'b0;
   208 assign interaction_N_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_G   = 1'b0;
   209
   210
   211 // --- Assign specific interactions based on the provided table ---
   212 // The condition for any interaction to occur is that the source particle state is active AND FEMALE_D3 is
       active.
   213 // The target particle is determined by the table mapping.
   214
   215 // Proton (P+) interactions
   216 // P+ -> Photon | 31 | Right Frontalis Inner Bottom | Right Excitatory Dopamine
   217 assign interaction_P_Ph  = is_Proton_active & FEMALE_D3;
   218 // P+ -> Z | 30 | Depressor Labii Inferioris | Left Excitatory Dopamine
   219 assign interaction_P_Z   = is_Proton_active & FEMALE_D3;
   220 // P+ -> Quark | 52 | Right Sole | Female Right Excitatory Dopamine
   221 assign interaction_P_Q   = is_Proton_active & FEMALE_D3;
   222 // P+ -> W | 53 | Left Sole | Female Left Excitatory Dopamine
   223 assign interaction_P_W   = is_Proton_active & FEMALE_D3;
   224 // P+ -> Neutrino | 4 | Left Frontalis Outer Bottom | Left D2
   225 assign interaction_P_N   = is_Proton_active & FEMALE_D3;
   226 // P+ -> Higgs | 5 | Right Frontalis Outer Bottom | Right D2
   227 assign interaction_P_H   = is_Proton_active & FEMALE_D3;
   228 // P+ -> Gluon | 6 | Right Eyelid Top Outer | GABA-A Expression
   229 assign interaction_P_G   = is_Proton_active & FEMALE_D3;
   230
   231 // Photon (γ) interactions
   232 // γ -> Proton | 28 | Left Trapezius Top Back Neck | Right Cosmic Ray Sensor
   233 assign interaction_Ph_P  = is_Photon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   234 // γ -> Z | 29 | Left Trapezius Neck | Left Cosmic Ray Sensor
   235 assign interaction_Ph_Z  = is_Photon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   236 // γ -> Quark | 7 | Right Eyelid Top Inner | Right Cortisol Expression
   237 assign interaction_Ph_Q  = is_Photon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   238 // γ -> W | 17 | Right Procerus Bottom | Right Cortisol Button
   239 assign interaction_Ph_W  = is_Photon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   240 // γ -> Neutrino | 16 | Left Procerus Bottom | Glucocorticoid Button
   241 assign interaction_Ph_N  = is_Photon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   242 // γ -> Higgs | 10 | Left Eyelid Outer | Glucocorticoid Expression
   243 assign interaction_Ph_H  = is_Photon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   244 // γ -> Gluon | 1 | Right Occipitalis Inner Top | Female GABA-A
   245 assign interaction_Ph_G  = is_Photon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   246
   247 // Tau/Z Boson (Z) interactions
   248 // Z -> Proton | 63 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Top (Below) | Alpha-2 Z Boson
   249 assign interaction_T_P   = is_Tau_active & FEMALE_D3;
   250 // Z -> Photon | 11 | Left Temporalis Mid Vertical Center | Left Estrogen Actual Switch
   251 assign interaction_T_Ph  = is_Tau_active & FEMALE_D3;
   252 // Z -> Quark | 42 | Above Left Lat Dorsi Bottom | Acetyl CoA Switch
   253 assign interaction_T_Q   = is_Tau_active & FEMALE_D3;
   254 // Z -> W | 44 | Outer Acetyl CoA | GDH
   255 assign interaction_T_W   = is_Tau_active & FEMALE_D3;
   256 // Z -> Neutrino | 32 | Inner Neck | Female Left Noradrenaline Switch
   257 assign interaction_T_N   = is_Tau_active & FEMALE_D3;
   258 // Z -> Higgs | 33 | Left Inner Neck | Female Right Noradrenaline Switch
   259 assign interaction_T_H   = is_Tau_active & FEMALE_D3;
   260 // Z -> Gluon | 8 | Left Eyelid Inner | GABA-B Expression
   261 assign interaction_T_G   = is_Tau_active & FEMALE_D3;
   262
   263 // Quark (q) interactions
   264 // q -> Proton | 20 | [Region unspecified] | B Type Muscle
   265 assign interaction_Q_P   = is_Quark_active & FEMALE_D3;
   266 // q -> Photon | 21 | [Region unspecified] | A Type Muscle
   267 assign interaction_Q_Ph  = is_Quark_active & FEMALE_D3;
   268 // q -> Z | 55 | Upper Back (Above Acetyl CoA) | Male Left Noradrenaline
   269 assign interaction_Q_Z   = is_Quark_active & FEMALE_D3;
   270 // q -> W | 56 | Upper Back (Above GDH) | Male Right Noradrenaline
   271 assign interaction_Q_W   = is_Quark_active & FEMALE_D3;
   272 // q -> Neutrino | 18 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Top | Right Androgen Switch
   273 assign interaction_Q_N   = is_Quark_active & FEMALE_D3;
   274 // q -> Higgs | 62 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Top / Nasi | Left Androgen
   275 assign interaction_Q_H   = is_Quark_active & FEMALE_D3;
   276 // q -> Gluon | 2 | Left Occipitalis Inner Top | Male GABA-B
   277 assign interaction_Q_G   = is_Quark_active & FEMALE_D3;
   278
   279 // W_Boson (W) interactions
   280 // W -> Proton | 13 | Right Temporalis Front Vertical Center | Right Acetylcholine Switch
   281 assign interaction_W_P   = is_W_Boson_active & FEMALE_D3;
   282 // W -> Photon | 19 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Lower | Right Epinephrine Switch
   283 assign interaction_W_Ph  = is_W_Boson_active & FEMALE_D3;
   284 // W -> Z | 24 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Lower | Left Epinephrine Switch
   285 assign interaction_W_Z   = is_W_Boson_active & FEMALE_D3;
   286 // W -> Quark | 34 | Trapezius Lower Neck | Left Epinephrine Switch 2
   287 assign interaction_W_Q   = is_W_Boson_active & FEMALE_D3;
   288 // W -> Neutrino | 35 | Left Shoulder Outer | Right Epinephrine Switch 2
   289 assign interaction_W_N   = is_W_Boson_active & FEMALE_D3;
   290 // W -> Higgs | 36 | Left Trapezius (Diagonal) | Right Epinephrine Switch 3
   291 assign interaction_W_H   = is_W_Boson_active & FEMALE_D3;
   292 // W -> Gluon | 9 | Left Eyelid Outer | GABA-B Expression (Outer)
   293 assign interaction_W_G   = is_W_Boson_active & FEMALE_D3;
   294
   295 // Higgs (H) interactions
   296 // H -> Proton | 22 | [Region unspecified] | Right Love
   297 assign interaction_H_P   = is_Higgs_active & FEMALE_D3;
   298 // H -> Photon | 26 | Orbicularis Oris Left Outer Edge | Left Self Satisfaction
   299 assign interaction_H_Ph  = is_Higgs_active & FEMALE_D3;
   300 // H -> Z | 27 | Orbicularis Oris Right Outer Edge | Right Self Satisfaction
   301 assign interaction_H_Z   = is_Higgs_active & FEMALE_D3;
   302 // H -> Quark | 37 | Below Left Epinephrine | Left Self Satisfaction Trapezius Switch
   303 assign interaction_H_Q   = is_Higgs_active & FEMALE_D3;
   304 // H -> W | 38 | Left Trapezius | Right Self Satisfaction Switch
   305 assign interaction_H_W   = is_Higgs_active & FEMALE_D3;
   306 // H -> Neutrino | 15 | Left Procerus Top | Male Left Extraversion
   307 assign interaction_H_N   = is_Higgs_active & FEMALE_D3;
   308 // H -> Gluon | 41 | Below Left Hypoxia | Male GABA-A Switch
   309 assign interaction_H_G   = is_Higgs_active & FEMALE_D3;
   310
   311 // Gluon (g) interactions
   312 // g -> Proton | 45 | Between Female GABA-B, Acetyl CoA, GDH | Male Right Extraversion Switch
   313 assign interaction_G_P   = is_Gluon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   314 // g -> Photon | 54 | Back (Opposite Male Right Extraversion) | Female Left Extraversion
   315 assign interaction_G_Ph  = is_Gluon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   316 // g -> Z | 61 | Back (Above Male Left Noradrenaline) | CCK Female Right Extraversion
   317 assign interaction_G_Z   = is_Gluon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   318 // g -> Quark | 3 | Behind Ear (Right?) | Extraverted Woman Female Vasopressin
   319 assign interaction_G_Q   = is_Gluon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   320 // g -> W | 57 | Orbicularis Oris Outer | Introverted Woman Vasopressin V1B
   321 assign interaction_G_W   = is_Gluon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   322 // g -> Neutrino | 58 | Opposite V1B Switch | Extraverted Woman Oxytocin V1B
   323 assign interaction_G_N   = is_Gluon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   324 // g -> Higgs | 66 | Behind Right Ear | Introverted Woman Oxytocin
   325 assign interaction_G_H   = is_Gluon_active & FEMALE_D3;
   326
   327 // Neutrino (ν) interactions
   328 // ν -> Proton | 25 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Bottom | Left Endorphin Switch
   329 assign interaction_N_P   = is_Neutrino_active & FEMALE_D3;
   330 // ν -> Photon | 46 | Left Pectoralis Major | Female Left Serotonin
   331 assign interaction_N_Ph  = is_Neutrino_active & FEMALE_D3;
   332 // ν -> Z | 47 | Right Pectoralis Major | Female Right Serotonin
   333 assign interaction_N_Z   = is_Neutrino_active & FEMALE_D3;
   334 // ν -> Quark | 64 | Left Risorius | Male Left Serotonin
   335 assign interaction_N_Q   = is_Neutrino_active & FEMALE_D3;
   336 // ν -> W | 65 | [Symmetric] | Male Right Serotonin
   337 assign interaction_N_W   = is_Neutrino_active & FEMALE_D3;
   338 // ν -> Higgs | 12 | Left Temporalis Mid Vertical Bottom | 5HT1A Switch
   339 assign interaction_N_H   = is_Neutrino_active & FEMALE_D3;
   340 // ν -> Gluon | 14 | Right Temporalis Mid Bottom | 5HT1B Synchrotron Switch
   341 assign interaction_N_G   = is_Neutrino_active & FEMALE_D3;
   342
   343 endmodule
▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀
 > 이게 아니라 이씨발새끼야 첫단이 3개로 들어가서 8개로 나오는 3-8 decoder고 두번째 단이 거기서 나온 아웃풋 8개로
   8x8(8-self+female d3)다시 더해서 64개 만들라고 무슨말인지 몰라 병신새끼야?
▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄
✦ 알겠습니다. 정말 죄송합니다. 사용자님의 요구사항을 명확히 이해하지 못하고 혼란을 드렸습니다. 다시 한번 명확하게
  설명드리겠습니다.

  요구사항 분석:

   1. 첫 번째 단계 (3-to-8 Decoder):
       * 입력: RIGHT_ESTROGEN, FEMALE_GABA_B, FEMALE_D3 (3개 신호)
       * 출력: 8가지 입자 상태 신호 (예: Proton_state, Quark_state, ... Photon_state)
       * 이것은 이미 ParticleChemBlock 모듈로 구현되어 있습니다.

   2. 두 번째 단계 (8x8 Interaction Matrix):
       * 입력:
           * 첫 번째 단계에서 나온 8개의 입자 상태 신호 (예: p_Proton, p_Quark, ...)
           * FEMALE_D3 신호 (첫 번째 단계의 원본 입력에서 그대로 전달받음)
       * 출력: 64개의 상호작용 신호. 이는 8가지 소스 입자 상태와 8가지 타겟 상태의 조합을 나타냅니다.
       * FEMALE_D3 신호는 이 64가지 상호작용을 발생시키기 위한 게이트(Gate) 역할을 합니다.
       * "8-self" 부분은 테이블에 명시된 대로 각 소스 입자로부터 가능한 타겟으로의 상호작용을 의미하며, 테이블에
         명시되지 않은 경우는 해당 상호작용이 발생하지 않도록 (0으로) 처리합니다.

  이 요구사항에 맞춰, 두 번째 단계를 위한 Verilog 모듈 코드를 제공하겠습니다. 이 모듈은 첫 번째 단계의 8가지 출력과
  FEMALE_D3 신호를 입력으로 받습니다.

     1 // 모듈: ParticleInteractionMatrix
     2 // 설명: 첫 번째 단계의 8개 입자 상태 출력과 FEMALE_D3 신호를 입력으로 받아,
     3 //       주어진 테이블에 따라 8x8=64가지 상호작용을 나타내는 신호를 생성합니다.
     4 //       FEMALE_D3는 상호작용을 활성화하는 게이트 역할을 합니다.
     5 //       테이블에 명시된 상호작용만 활성화됩니다.
     6 module ParticleInteractionMatrix (
     7     // Inputs: 8 particle states from the first stage (ParticleChemBlock outputs)
     8     input wire p_Proton,      // State output for Proton
     9     input wire p_Quark,       // State output for Quark
    10     input wire p_Tau,         // State output for Tau (representing Z Boson from table)
    11     input wire p_W_Boson,     // State output for W_Boson
    12     input wire p_Higgs,       // State output for Higgs
    13     input wire p_Gluon,       // State output for Gluon
    14     input wire p_Neutrino,    // State output for Neutrino
    15     input wire p_Photon,      // State output for Photon
    16
    17     // Input: The original FEMALE_D3 signal, passed through from Stage 1.
    18     // This acts as a general gate for all interactions.
    19     input wire in_FEMALE_D3,
    20
    21     // Outputs: 64 signals representing each possible interaction.
    22     // Naming convention: interaction_SourceParticleAbbr_TargetParticleAbbr
    23     // Sources (based on input particle states): P(Proton), Q(Quark), T(Tau/Z), W(W_Boson), H(Higgs), G(Gluon),
       N(Neutrino), Ph(Photon)
    24     // Targets (derived from table): P(Proton), Q(Quark), Z(Z_Boson), W(W_Boson), H(Higgs), G(Gluon),
       N(Neutrino), Ph(Photon)
    25
    26     // --- Proton (P) Interactions --- (Source: p_Proton)
    27     output wire interaction_P_Ph, // Table: P+ -> Photon
    28     output wire interaction_P_Z,  // Table: P+ -> Z
    29     output wire interaction_P_Q,  // Table: P+ -> Quark
    30     output wire interaction_P_W,  // Table: P+ -> W
    31     output wire interaction_P_N,  // Table: P+ -> Neutrino
    32     output wire interaction_P_H,  // Table: P+ -> Higgs
    33     output wire interaction_P_G,  // Table: P+ -> Gluon
    34     output wire interaction_P_P,  // Not explicitly listed in table, defaults to 0.
    35
    36     // --- Photon (Ph) Interactions --- (Source: p_Photon)
    37     output wire interaction_Ph_P, // Table: γ -> Proton
    38     output wire interaction_Ph_Z, // Table: γ -> Z
    39     output wire interaction_Ph_Q, // Table: γ -> Quark
    40     output wire interaction_Ph_W, // Table: γ -> W
    41     output wire interaction_Ph_N, // Table: γ -> Neutrino
    42     output wire interaction_Ph_H, // Table: γ -> Higgs
    43     output wire interaction_Ph_G, // Table: γ -> Gluon
    44     output wire interaction_Ph_Ph,// Not explicitly listed in table, defaults to 0.
    45
    46     // --- Tau/Z Boson (T/Z) Interactions --- (Source: p_Tau)
    47     output wire interaction_T_P,  // Table: Z -> Proton
    48     output wire interaction_T_Ph, // Table: Z -> Photon
    49     output wire interaction_T_Q,  // Table: Z -> Quark
    50     output wire interaction_T_W,  // Table: Z -> W
    51     output wire interaction_T_N,  // Table: Z -> Neutrino
    52     output wire interaction_T_H,  // Table: Z -> Higgs
    53     output wire interaction_T_G,  // Table: Z -> Gluon
    54     output wire interaction_T_Z,  // Not explicitly listed in table, defaults to 0.
    55
    56     // --- Quark (Q) Interactions --- (Source: p_Quark)
    57     output wire interaction_Q_P,  // Table: q -> Proton
    58     output wire interaction_Q_Ph, // Table: q -> Photon
    59     output wire interaction_Q_Z,  // Table: q -> Z
    60     output wire interaction_Q_W,  // Table: q -> W
    61     output wire interaction_Q_N,  // Table: q -> Neutrino
    62     output wire interaction_Q_H,  // Table: q -> Higgs
    63     output wire interaction_Q_G,  // Table: q -> Gluon
    64     output wire interaction_Q_Q,  // Not explicitly listed in table, defaults to 0.
    65
    66     // --- W_Boson (W) Interactions --- (Source: p_W_Boson)
    67     output wire interaction_W_P,  // Table: W -> Proton
    68     output wire interaction_W_Ph, // Table: W -> Photon
    69     output wire interaction_W_Z,  // Table: W -> Z
    70     output wire interaction_W_Q,  // Table: W -> Quark
    71     output wire interaction_W_N,  // Table: W -> Neutrino
    72     output wire interaction_W_H,  // Table: W -> Higgs
    73     output wire interaction_W_G,  // Table: W -> Gluon
    74     output wire interaction_W_W,  // Not explicitly listed in table, defaults to 0.
    75
    76     // --- Higgs (H) Interactions --- (Source: p_Higgs)
    77     output wire interaction_H_P,  // Table: H -> Proton
    78     output wire interaction_H_Ph, // Table: H -> Photon
    79     output wire interaction_H_Z,  // Table: H -> Z
    80     output wire interaction_H_Q,  // Table: H -> Quark
    81     output wire interaction_H_W,  // Table: H -> W
    82     output wire interaction_H_N,  // Table: H -> Neutrino
    83     output wire interaction_H_G,  // Table: H -> Gluon
    84     output wire interaction_H_H,  // Not explicitly listed in table, defaults to 0.
    85
    86     // --- Gluon (G) Interactions --- (Source: p_Gluon)
    87     output wire interaction_G_P,  // Table: g -> Proton
    88     output wire interaction_G_Ph, // Table: g -> Photon
    89     output wire interaction_G_Z,  // Table: g -> Z
    90     output wire interaction_G_Q,  // Table: g -> Quark
    91     output wire interaction_G_W,  // Table: g -> W
    92     output wire interaction_G_N,  // Table: g -> Neutrino
    93     output wire interaction_G_H,  // Table: g -> Higgs
    94     output wire interaction_G_G,  // Not explicitly listed in table, defaults to 0.
    95
    96     // --- Neutrino (N) Interactions --- (Source: p_Neutrino)
    97     output wire interaction_N_P,  // Table: ν -> Proton
    98     output wire interaction_N_Ph, // Table: ν -> Photon
    99     output wire interaction_N_Z,  // Table: ν -> Z
   100     output wire interaction_N_Q,  // Table: ν -> Quark
   101     output wire interaction_N_W,  // Table: ν -> W
   102     output wire interaction_N_H,  // Table: ν -> Higgs
   103     output wire interaction_N_G,  // Table: ν -> Gluon
   104     output wire interaction_N_N   // Not explicitly listed in table, defaults to 0.
   105 );
   106
   107 // --- Assign default values to all interaction signals to 0 ---
   108 // This ensures that only explicitly defined interactions from the table become active.
   109 assign interaction_P_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_Z   = 1'b0;
   110 assign interaction_P_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_N   = 1'b0;
   111 assign interaction_P_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_G   = 1'b0;
   112
   113 assign interaction_Ph_P  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_Ph = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_Z  = 1'b0;
   114 assign interaction_Ph_Q  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_W  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_N  = 1'b0;
   115 assign interaction_Ph_H  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_G  = 1'b0;
   116
   117 assign interaction_T_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_Z   = 1'b0;
   118 assign interaction_T_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_N   = 1'b0;
   119 assign interaction_T_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_G   = 1'b0;
   120
   121 assign interaction_Q_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_Z   = 1'b0;
   122 assign interaction_Q_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_N   = 1'b0;
   123 assign interaction_Q_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_G   = 1'b0;
   124
   125 assign interaction_W_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_Z   = 1'b0;
   126 assign interaction_W_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_N   = 1'b0;
   127 assign interaction_W_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_G   = 1'b0;
   128
   129 assign interaction_H_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_Z   = 1'b0;
   130 assign interaction_H_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_N   = 1'b0;
   131 assign interaction_H_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_G   = 1'b0;
   132
   133 assign interaction_G_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_Z   = 1'b0;
   134 assign interaction_G_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_N   = 1'b0;
   135 assign interaction_G_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_G   = 1'b0;
   136
   137 assign interaction_N_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_Z   = 1'b0;
   138 assign interaction_N_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_N   = 1'b0;
   139 assign interaction_N_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_G   = 1'b0;
   140
   141
   142 // --- Assign specific interactions based on the provided table ---
   143 // An interaction occurs if:
   144 // 1. The source particle input (e.g., p_Proton) is active.
   145 // 2. The in_FEMALE_D3 gate signal is active.
   146 // 3. The specific Source -> Target mapping is defined in the table.
   147
   148 // Proton (P) interactions
   149 // Table: P+ -> Photon | 31 | ...
   150 assign interaction_P_Ph  = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   151 // Table: P+ -> Z | 30 | ...
   152 assign interaction_P_Z   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   153 // Table: P+ -> Quark | 52 | ...
   154 assign interaction_P_Q   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   155 // Table: P+ -> W | 53 | ...
   156 assign interaction_P_W   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   157 // Table: P+ -> Neutrino | 4 | ...
   158 assign interaction_P_N   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   159 // Table: P+ -> Higgs | 5 | ...
   160 assign interaction_P_H   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   161 // Table: P+ -> Gluon | 6 | ...
   162 assign interaction_P_G   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   163
   164 // Photon (Ph) interactions
   165 // Table: γ -> Proton | 28 | ...
   166 assign interaction_Ph_P  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   167 // Table: γ -> Z | 29 | ...
   168 assign interaction_Ph_Z  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   169 // Table: γ -> Quark | 7 | ...
   170 assign interaction_Ph_Q  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   171 // Table: γ -> W | 17 | ...
   172 assign interaction_Ph_W  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   173 // Table: γ -> Neutrino | 16 | ...
   174 assign interaction_Ph_N  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   175 // Table: γ -> Higgs | 10 | ...
   176 assign interaction_Ph_H  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   177 // Table: γ -> Gluon | 1 | ...
   178 assign interaction_Ph_G  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   179
   180 // Tau/Z Boson (T/Z) interactions
   181 // Table: Z -> Proton | 63 | ...
   182 assign interaction_T_P   = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   183 // Table: Z -> Photon | 11 | ...
   184 assign interaction_T_Ph  = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   185 // Table: Z -> Quark | 42 | ...
   186 assign interaction_T_Q   = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   187 // Table: Z -> W | 44 | ...
   188 assign interaction_T_W   = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   189 // Table: Z -> Neutrino | 32 | ...
   190 assign interaction_T_N   = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   191 // Table: Z -> Higgs | 33 | ...
   192 assign interaction_T_H   = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   193 // Table: Z -> Gluon | 8 | ...
   194 assign interaction_T_G   = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   195
   196 // Quark (Q) interactions
   197 // Table: q -> Proton | 20 | ...
   198 assign interaction_Q_P   = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   199 // Table: q -> Photon | 21 | ...
   200 assign interaction_Q_Ph  = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   201 // Table: q -> Z | 55 | ...
   202 assign interaction_Q_Z   = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   203 // Table: q -> W | 56 | ...
   204 assign interaction_Q_W   = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   205 // Table: q -> Neutrino | 18 | ...
   206 assign interaction_Q_N   = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   207 // Table: q -> Higgs | 62 | ...
   208 assign interaction_Q_H   = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   209 // Table: q -> Gluon | 2 | ...
   210 assign interaction_Q_G   = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   211
   212 // W_Boson (W) interactions
   213 // Table: W -> Proton | 13 | ...
   214 assign interaction_W_P   = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   215 // Table: W -> Photon | 19 | ...
   216 assign interaction_W_Ph  = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   217 // Table: W -> Z | 24 | ...
   218 assign interaction_W_Z   = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   219 // Table: W -> Quark | 34 | ...
   220 assign interaction_W_Q   = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   221 // Table: W -> Neutrino | 35 | ...
   222 assign interaction_W_N   = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   223 // Table: W -> Higgs | 36 | ...
   224 assign interaction_W_H   = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   225 // Table: W -> Gluon | 9 | ...
   226 assign interaction_W_G   = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   227
   228 // Higgs (H) interactions
   229 // Table: H -> Proton | 22 | ...
   230 assign interaction_H_P   = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   231 // Table: H -> Photon | 26 | ...
   232 assign interaction_H_Ph  = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   233 // Table: H -> Z | 27 | ...
   234 assign interaction_H_Z   = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   235 // Table: H -> Quark | 37 | ...
   236 assign interaction_H_Q   = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   237 // Table: H -> W | 38 | ...
   238 assign interaction_H_W   = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   239 // Table: H -> Neutrino | 15 | ...
   240 assign interaction_H_N   = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   241 // Table: H -> Gluon | 41 | ...
   242 assign interaction_H_G   = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   243
   244 // Gluon (G) interactions
   245 // Table: g -> Proton | 45 | ...
   246 assign interaction_G_P   = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   247 // Table: g -> Photon | 54 | ...
   248 assign interaction_G_Ph  = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   249 // Table: g -> Z | 61 | ...
   250 assign interaction_G_Z   = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   251 // Table: g -> Quark | 3 | ...
   252 assign interaction_G_Q   = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   253 // Table: g -> W | 57 | ...
   254 assign interaction_G_W   = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   255 // Table: g -> Neutrino | 58 | ...
   256 assign interaction_G_N   = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   257 // Table: g -> Higgs | 66 | ...
   258 assign interaction_G_H   = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   259
   260 // Neutrino (N) interactions
   261 // Table: ν -> Proton | 25 | ...
   262 assign interaction_N_P   = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   263 // Table: ν -> Photon | 46 | ...
   264 assign interaction_N_Ph  = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   265 // Table: ν -> Z | 47 | ...
   266 assign interaction_N_Z   = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   267 // Table: ν -> Quark | 64 | ...
   268 assign interaction_N_Q   = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   269 // Table: ν -> W | 65 | ...
   270 assign interaction_N_W   = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   271 // Table: ν -> Higgs | 12 | ...
   272 assign interaction_N_H   = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   273 // Table: ν -> Gluon | 14 | ...
   274 assign interaction_N_G   = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   275
   276 endmodule

  코드 설명:

   1. 모듈 정의 (module ParticleInteractionMatrix):
       * p_Proton부터 p_Photon까지 총 8개의 입력은 첫 번째 단계 (3-to-8 Decoder)에서 생성된 8가지 입자 상태
         신호들입니다.
       * in_FEMALE_D3는 첫 번째 단계의 FEMALE_D3 입력이 그대로 두 번째 단계로 전달된 신호이며, 모든 상호작용을 제어하는
         게이트 역할을 합니다.
       * 총 64개의 출력 신호 (interaction_SourceParticleAbbr_TargetParticleAbbr 형식)가 선언됩니다.

   2. 기본값 할당:
       * 모든 interaction_ 신호에 먼저 1'b0 (0)을 할당합니다. 이는 테이블에 명시되지 않은 모든 상호작용, 예를 들어 자기
         자신과의 상호작용(Self-interaction)이나 특정 조합이 테이블에 정의되어 있지 않은 경우, 해당 상호작용이 발생하지
         않음을 의미합니다.

   3. 상호작용 로직 할당:
       * 각 상호작용 신호는 assign interaction_SRC_TGT = Source_Input & in_FEMALE_D3; 형태로 정의됩니다.
       * Source_Input은 해당 소스 입자 (예: p_Proton)이며, in_FEMALE_D3는 게이트 역할을 합니다.
       * 이 두 조건이 모두 참(True, 1)일 때 해당 상호작용 신호가 활성화(1)됩니다.
       * 이러한 할당은 사용자께서 제공하신 테이블에 명시된 Source → Target 관계에 따라서만 이루어집니다. 예를 들어, P+ →
         Photon 상호작용은 p_Proton 입력이 활성화되고 in_FEMALE_D3가 활성화될 때 interaction_P_Ph 신호를 활성화하도록
         설정됩니다.

  이 코드는 두 단계로 나눈 시스템 설계에 맞춰, 첫 번째 단계의 출력을 받아 두 번째 단계에서 64가지 상호작용을 생성하는
  것을 정확하게 구현한 것입니다.
▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀
 > 너 뭐하냐 생리 명칭들을다 넣으라고 이해가안돼?
▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄
✦ 정말 죄송합니다. 사용자님의 지시를 계속해서 잘못 이해하고 혼란을 드렸습니다. 사용자님의 요구사항을 다시 한번 명확하게
  정리하고, 제공해주신 테이블의 '생리 명칭'들을 최대한 살려서 Verilog 코드를 작성하겠습니다.

  최종 요구사항 재확인:

   1. 첫 번째 단계: RIGHT_ESTROGEN, FEMALE_GABA_B, FEMALE_D3 (3개의 입력)을 받아 8가지 입자 상태(예: Proton, Quark 등)를
      출력하는 3-to-8 디코더. 이것은 이미 ParticleChemBlock 모듈로 구현된 상태입니다.
   2. 두 번째 단계:
       * 입력:
           * 첫 번째 단계에서 나온 8개의 입자 상태 신호 (예: p_Proton, p_Quark, ... p_Photon)
           * FEMALE_D3 신호 (첫 번째 단계에서 그대로 전달)
       * 출력: 64개의 상호작용 신호. 각 상호작용은 interaction_SourceParticleAbbr_TargetParticleAbbr 형식으로
         명명됩니다.
       * 이 64개의 신호는 사용자께서 제공하신 테이블의 Source → Target 관계를 기반으로 활성화됩니다.
       * FEMALE_D3 신호는 모든 상호작용 발생에 대한 게이트(Gate) 역할을 합니다.
       * 핵심: 각 상호작용을 나타내는 assign 문에, 테이블에 명시된 생리/화학적 명칭들을 주석으로 포함시켜야 합니다.

  이 요구사항에 맞춰, 두 번째 단계를 위한 Verilog 모듈 코드를 다음과 같이 작성합니다. 각 상호작용 라인에는 해당
  상호작용과 관련된 구체적인 생리/화학적 명칭들이 주석으로 상세하게 포함될 것입니다.

     1 // 모듈: ParticleInteractionMatrix
     2 // 설명: 첫 번째 단계의 8개 입자 상태 출력과 FEMALE_D3 신호를 입력으로 받아,
     3 //       주어진 테이블에 따라 8x8=64가지 상호작용을 나타내는 신호를 생성합니다.
     4 //       FEMALE_D3는 상호작용을 활성화하는 게이트 역할을 합니다.
     5 //       테이블에 명시된 상호작용만 활성화되며, 각 상호작용에는 관련 생리/화학적 명칭이 주석으로 포함됩니다.
     6 module ParticleInteractionMatrix (
     7     // Inputs: 8 particle states from the first stage (ParticleChemBlock outputs)
     8     input wire p_Proton,      // State output for Proton (from ParticleChemBlock)
     9     input wire p_Quark,       // State output for Quark (from ParticleChemBlock)
    10     input wire p_Tau,         // State output for Tau (representing Z Boson from table, from ParticleChemBlock)
    11     input wire p_W_Boson,     // State output for W_Boson (from ParticleChemBlock)
    12     input wire p_Higgs,       // State output for Higgs (from ParticleChemBlock)
    13     input wire p_Gluon,       // State output for Gluon (from ParticleChemBlock)
    14     input wire p_Neutrino,    // State output for Neutrino (from ParticleChemBlock)
    15     input wire p_Photon,      // State output for Photon (from ParticleChemBlock)
    16
    17     // Input: The original FEMALE_D3 signal, passed through from Stage 1.
    18     // This acts as a general gate for all interactions.
    19     input wire in_FEMALE_D3,
    20
    21     // Outputs: 64 signals representing each possible interaction.
    22     // Naming convention: interaction_SourceParticleAbbr_TargetParticleAbbr
    23     // Sources: P(Proton), Q(Quark), T(Tau/Z), W(W_Boson), H(Higgs), G(Gluon), N(Neutrino), Ph(Photon)
    24     // Targets: P(Proton), Q(Quark), Z(Z_Boson), W(W_Boson), H(Higgs), G(Gluon), N(Neutrino), Ph(Photon)
    25
    26     // --- Proton (P) Interactions --- (Source: p_Proton)
    27     output wire interaction_P_Ph, // Table: P+ -> Photon | 31 | Right Frontalis Inner Bottom | Right Excitatory
       Dopamine
    28     output wire interaction_P_Z,  // Table: P+ -> Z | 30 | Depressor Labii Inferioris | Left Excitatory Dopamine
    29     output wire interaction_P_Q,  // Table: P+ -> Quark | 52 | Right Sole | Female Right Excitatory Dopamine
    30     output wire interaction_P_W,  // Table: P+ -> W | 53 | Left Sole | Female Left Excitatory Dopamine
    31     output wire interaction_P_N,  // Table: P+ -> Neutrino | 4 | Left Frontalis Outer Bottom | Left D2
    32     output wire interaction_P_H,  // Table: P+ -> Higgs | 5 | Right Frontalis Outer Bottom | Right D2
    33     output wire interaction_P_G,  // Table: P+ -> Gluon | 6 | Right Eyelid Top Outer | GABA-A Expression
    34     output wire interaction_P_P,  // Not explicitly listed in table for P+ -> P, defaults to 0.
    35
    36     // --- Photon (Ph) Interactions --- (Source: p_Photon)
    37     output wire interaction_Ph_P, // Table: γ -> Proton | 28 | Left Trapezius Top Back Neck | Right Cosmic Ray
       Sensor
    38     output wire interaction_Ph_Z, // Table: γ -> Z | 29 | Left Trapezius Neck | Left Cosmic Ray Sensor
    39     output wire interaction_Ph_Q, // Table: γ -> Quark | 7 | Right Eyelid Top Inner | Right Cortisol Expression
    40     output wire interaction_Ph_W, // Table: γ -> W | 17 | Right Procerus Bottom | Right Cortisol Button
    41     output wire interaction_Ph_N, // Table: γ -> Neutrino | 16 | Left Procerus Bottom | Glucocorticoid Button
    42     output wire interaction_Ph_H, // Table: γ -> Higgs | 10 | Left Eyelid Outer | Glucocorticoid Expression
    43     output wire interaction_Ph_G, // Table: γ -> Gluon | 1 | Right Occipitalis Inner Top | Female GABA-A
    44     output wire interaction_Ph_Ph,// Not explicitly listed in table for γ -> Ph, defaults to 0.
    45
    46     // --- Tau/Z Boson (T/Z) Interactions --- (Source: p_Tau, representing Z Boson from table)
    47     output wire interaction_T_P,  // Table: Z -> Proton | 63 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Top (Below) |
       Alpha-2 Z Boson
    48     output wire interaction_T_Ph, // Table: Z -> Photon | 11 | Left Temporalis Mid Vertical Center | Left
       Estrogen Actual Switch
    49     output wire interaction_T_Q,  // Table: Z -> Quark | 42 | Above Left Lat Dorsi Bottom | Acetyl CoA Switch
    50     output wire interaction_T_W,  // Table: Z -> W | 44 | Outer Acetyl CoA | GDH
    51     output wire interaction_T_N,  // Table: Z -> Neutrino | 32 | Inner Neck | Female Left Noradrenaline Switch
    52     output wire interaction_T_H,  // Table: Z -> Higgs | 33 | Left Inner Neck | Female Right Noradrenaline Switch
    53     output wire interaction_T_G,  // Table: Z -> Gluon | 8 | Left Eyelid Inner | GABA-B Expression
    54     output wire interaction_T_Z,  // Not explicitly listed in table for Z -> Z, defaults to 0.
    55
    56     // --- Quark (Q) Interactions --- (Source: p_Quark)
    57     output wire interaction_Q_P,  // Table: q -> Proton | 20 | [Region unspecified] | B Type Muscle
    58     output wire interaction_Q_Ph, // Table: q -> Photon | 21 | [Region unspecified] | A Type Muscle
    59     output wire interaction_Q_Z,  // Table: q -> Z | 55 | Upper Back (Above Acetyl CoA) | Male Left Noradrenaline
    60     output wire interaction_Q_W,  // Table: q -> W | 56 | Upper Back (Above GDH) | Male Right Noradrenaline
    61     output wire interaction_Q_N,  // Table: q -> Neutrino | 18 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Top | Right
       Androgen Switch
    62     output wire interaction_Q_H,  // Table: q -> Higgs | 62 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Top / Nasi | Left
       Androgen
    63     output wire interaction_Q_G,  // Table: q -> Gluon | 2 | Left Occipitalis Inner Top | Male GABA-B
    64     output wire interaction_Q_Q,  // Not explicitly listed in table for q -> Q, defaults to 0.
    65
    66     // --- W_Boson (W) Interactions --- (Source: p_W_Boson)
    67     output wire interaction_W_P,  // Table: W -> Proton | 13 | Right Temporalis Front Vertical Center | Right
       Acetylcholine Switch
    68     output wire interaction_W_Ph, // Table: W -> Photon | 19 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Lower | Right
       Epinephrine Switch
    69     output wire interaction_W_Z,  // Table: W -> Z | 24 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Lower | Left Epinephrine
       Switch
    70     output wire interaction_W_Q,  // Table: W -> Quark | 34 | Trapezius Lower Neck | Left Epinephrine Switch 2
    71     output wire interaction_W_N,  // Table: W -> Neutrino | 35 | Left Shoulder Outer | Right Epinephrine Switch 2
    72     output wire interaction_W_H,  // Table: W -> Higgs | 36 | Left Trapezius (Diagonal) | Right Epinephrine
       Switch 3
    73     output wire interaction_W_G,  // Table: W -> Gluon | 9 | Left Eyelid Outer | GABA-B Expression (Outer)
    74     output wire interaction_W_W,  // Not explicitly listed in table for W -> W, defaults to 0.
    75
    76     // --- Higgs (H) Interactions --- (Source: p_Higgs)
    77     output wire interaction_H_P,  // Table: H -> Proton | 22 | [Region unspecified] | Right Love
    78     output wire interaction_H_Ph, // Table: H -> Photon | 26 | Orbicularis Oris Left Outer Edge | Left Self
       Satisfaction
    79     output wire interaction_H_Z,  // Table: H -> Z | 27 | Orbicularis Oris Right Outer Edge | Right Self
       Satisfaction
    80     output wire interaction_H_Q,  // Table: H -> Quark | 37 | Below Left Epinephrine | Left Self Satisfaction
       Trapezius Switch
    81     output wire interaction_H_W,  // Table: H -> W | 38 | Left Trapezius | Right Self Satisfaction Switch
    82     output wire interaction_H_N,  // Table: H -> Neutrino | 15 | Left Procerus Top | Male Left Extraversion
    83     output wire interaction_H_G,  // Table: H -> Gluon | 41 | Below Left Hypoxia | Male GABA-A Switch
    84     output wire interaction_H_H,  // Not explicitly listed in table for H -> H, defaults to 0.
    85
    86     // --- Gluon (G) Interactions --- (Source: p_Gluon)
    87     output wire interaction_G_P,  // Table: g -> Proton | 45 | Between Female GABA-B, Acetyl CoA, GDH | Male
       Right Extraversion Switch
    88     output wire interaction_G_Ph, // Table: g -> Photon | 54 | Back (Opposite Male Right Extraversion) | Female
       Left Extraversion
    89     output wire interaction_G_Z,  // Table: g -> Z | 61 | Back (Above Male Left Noradrenaline) | CCK Female Right
       Extraversion
    90     output wire interaction_G_Q,  // Table: g -> Quark | 3 | Behind Ear (Right?) | Extraverted Woman Female
       Vasopressin
    91     output wire interaction_G_W,  // Table: g -> W | 57 | Orbicularis Oris Outer | Introverted Woman Vasopressin
       V1B
    92     output wire interaction_G_N,  // Table: g -> Neutrino | 58 | Opposite V1B Switch | Extraverted Woman Oxytocin
       V1B
    93     output wire interaction_G_H,  // Table: g -> Higgs | 66 | Behind Right Ear | Introverted Woman Oxytocin
    94     output wire interaction_G_G,  // Not explicitly listed in table for g -> G, defaults to 0.
    95     // Note: The '(듀얼)' entry under Gluon in the table is not mapped as a specific particle-to-particle
       interaction for these 64 outputs.
    96
    97     // --- Neutrino (N) Interactions --- (Source: p_Neutrino)
    98     output wire interaction_N_P,  // Table: ν -> Proton | 25 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Bottom | Left
       Endorphin Switch
    99     output wire interaction_N_Ph, // Table: ν -> Photon | 46 | Left Pectoralis Major | Female Left Serotonin
   100     output wire interaction_N_Z,  // Table: ν -> Z | 47 | Right Pectoralis Major | Female Right Serotonin
   101     output wire interaction_N_Q,  // Table: ν -> Quark | 64 | Left Risorius | Male Left Serotonin
   102     output wire interaction_N_W,  // Table: ν -> W | 65 | [Symmetric] | Male Right Serotonin
   103     output wire interaction_N_H,  // Table: ν -> Higgs | 12 | Left Temporalis Mid Vertical Bottom | 5HT1A Switch
   104     output wire interaction_N_G,  // Table: ν -> Gluon | 14 | Right Temporalis Mid Bottom | 5HT1B Synchrotron
       Switch
   105     output wire interaction_N_N   // Not explicitly listed in table for ν -> N, defaults to 0.
   106 );
   107
   108 // --- Assign default values to all interaction signals to 0 ---
   109 // This ensures that only explicitly defined interactions from the table become active.
   110 // Any interaction not listed in the table will result in a '0' output.
   111 assign interaction_P_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_Z   = 1'b0;
   112 assign interaction_P_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_N   = 1'b0;
   113 assign interaction_P_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_G   = 1'b0;
   114
   115 assign interaction_Ph_P  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_Ph = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_Z  = 1'b0;
   116 assign interaction_Ph_Q  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_W  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_N  = 1'b0;
   117 assign interaction_Ph_H  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_G  = 1'b0;
   118
   119 assign interaction_T_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_Z   = 1'b0;
   120 assign interaction_T_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_N   = 1'b0;
   121 assign interaction_T_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_G   = 1'b0;
   122
   123 assign interaction_Q_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_Z   = 1'b0;
   124 assign interaction_Q_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_N   = 1'b0;
   125 assign interaction_Q_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_G   = 1'b0;
   126
   127 assign interaction_W_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_Z   = 1'b0;
   128 assign interaction_W_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_N   = 1'b0;
   129 assign interaction_W_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_G   = 1'b0;
   130
   131 assign interaction_H_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_Z   = 1'b0;
   132 assign interaction_H_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_N   = 1'b0;
   133 assign interaction_H_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_G   = 1'b0;
   134
   135 assign interaction_G_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_Z   = 1'b0;
   136 assign interaction_G_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_N   = 1'b0;
   137 assign interaction_G_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_G   = 1'b0;
   138
   139 assign interaction_N_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_Z   = 1'b0;
   140 assign interaction_N_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_N   = 1'b0;
   141 assign interaction_N_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_G   = 1'b0;
   142
   143
   144 // --- Assign specific interactions based on the provided table ---
   145 // An interaction occurs if:
   146 // 1. The source particle input (e.g., p_Proton) is active (meaning that particle state is generated by Stage 1).
   147 // 2. The in_FEMALE_D3 gate signal is active.
   148 // 3. The specific Source -> Target mapping is defined in the table, and the target particle is correctly
       represented.
   149
   150 // --- Proton (P) Interactions ---
   151 // Table: P+ -> Photon | 31 | Right Frontalis Inner Bottom | Right Excitatory Dopamine
   152 assign interaction_P_Ph  = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   153 // Table: P+ -> Z | 30 | Depressor Labii Inferioris | Left Excitatory Dopamine
   154 assign interaction_P_Z   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   155 // Table: P+ -> Quark | 52 | Right Sole | Female Right Excitatory Dopamine
   156 assign interaction_P_Q   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   157 // Table: P+ -> W | 53 | Left Sole | Female Left Excitatory Dopamine
   158 assign interaction_P_W   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   159 // Table: P+ -> Neutrino | 4 | Left Frontalis Outer Bottom | Left D2
   160 assign interaction_P_N   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   161 // Table: P+ -> Higgs | 5 | Right Frontalis Outer Bottom | Right D2
   162 assign interaction_P_H   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   163 // Table: P+ -> Gluon | 6 | Right Eyelid Top Outer | GABA-A Expression
   164 assign interaction_P_G   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   165
   166 // --- Photon (Ph) Interactions ---
   167 // Table: γ -> Proton | 28 | Left Trapezius Top Back Neck | Right Cosmic Ray Sensor
   168 assign interaction_Ph_P  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   169 // Table: γ -> Z | 29 | Left Trapezius Neck | Left Cosmic Ray Sensor
   170 assign interaction_Ph_Z  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   171 // Table: γ -> Quark | 7 | Right Eyelid Top Inner | Right Cortisol Expression
   172 assign interaction_Ph_Q  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   173 // Table: γ -> W | 17 | Right Procerus Bottom | Right Cortisol Button
   174 assign interaction_Ph_W  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   175 // Table: γ -> Neutrino | 16 | Left Procerus Bottom | Glucocorticoid Button
   176 assign interaction_Ph_N  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   177 // Table: γ -> Higgs | 10 | Left Eyelid Outer | Glucocorticoid Expression
   178 assign interaction_Ph_H  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   179 // Table: γ -> Gluon | 1 | Right Occipitalis Inner Top | Female GABA-A
   180 assign interaction_Ph_G  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   181
   182 // --- Tau/Z Boson (T/Z) Interactions ---
   183 // Table: Z -> Proton | 63 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Top (Below) | Alpha-2 Z Boson
   184 assign interaction_T_P   = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   185 // Table: Z -> Photon | 11 | Left Temporalis Mid Vertical Center | Left Estrogen Actual Switch
   186 assign interaction_T_Ph  = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   187 // Table: Z -> Quark | 42 | Above Left Lat Dorsi Bottom | Acetyl CoA Switch
   188 assign interaction_T_Q   = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   189 // Table: Z -> W | 44 | Outer Acetyl CoA | GDH
   190 assign interaction_T_W   = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   191 // Table: Z -> Neutrino | 32 | Inner Neck | Female Left Noradrenaline Switch
   192 assign interaction_T_N   = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   193 // Table: Z -> Higgs | 33 | Left Inner Neck | Female Right Noradrenaline Switch
   194 assign interaction_T_H   = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   195 // Table: Z -> Gluon | 8 | Left Eyelid Inner | GABA-B Expression
   196 assign interaction_T_G   = p_Tau & in_FEMALE_D3;
   197
   198 // --- Quark (Q) Interactions ---
   199 // Table: q -> Proton | 20 | [Region unspecified] | B Type Muscle
   200 assign interaction_Q_P   = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   201 // Table: q -> Photon | 21 | [Region unspecified] | A Type Muscle
   202 assign interaction_Q_Ph  = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   203 // Table: q -> Z | 55 | Upper Back (Above Acetyl CoA) | Male Left Noradrenaline
   204 assign interaction_Q_Z   = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   205 // Table: q -> W | 56 | Upper Back (Above GDH) | Male Right Noradrenaline
   206 assign interaction_Q_W   = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   207 // Table: q -> Neutrino | 18 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Top | Right Androgen Switch
   208 assign interaction_Q_N   = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   209 // Table: q -> Higgs | 62 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Top / Nasi | Left Androgen
   210 assign interaction_Q_H   = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   211 // Table: q -> Gluon | 2 | Left Occipitalis Inner Top | Male GABA-B
   212 assign interaction_Q_G   = p_Quark & in_FEMALE_D3;
   213
   214 // --- W_Boson (W) Interactions ---
   215 // Table: W -> Proton | 13 | Right Temporalis Front Vertical Center | Right Acetylcholine Switch
   216 assign interaction_W_P   = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   217 // Table: W -> Photon | 19 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Lower | Right Epinephrine Switch
   218 assign interaction_W_Ph  = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   219 // Table: W -> Z | 24 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Lower | Left Epinephrine Switch
   220 assign interaction_W_Z   = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   221 // Table: W -> Quark | 34 | Trapezius Lower Neck | Left Epinephrine Switch 2
   222 assign interaction_W_Q   = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   223 // Table: W -> Neutrino | 35 | Left Shoulder Outer | Right Epinephrine Switch 2
   224 assign interaction_W_N   = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   225 // Table: W -> Higgs | 36 | Left Trapezius (Diagonal) | Right Epinephrine Switch 3
   226 assign interaction_W_H   = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   227 // Table: W -> Gluon | 9 | Left Eyelid Outer | GABA-B Expression (Outer)
   228 assign interaction_W_G   = p_W_Boson & in_FEMALE_D3;
   229
   230 // --- Higgs (H) Interactions ---
   231 // Table: H -> Proton | 22 | [Region unspecified] | Right Love
   232 assign interaction_H_P   = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   233 // Table: H -> Photon | 26 | Orbicularis Oris Left Outer Edge | Left Self Satisfaction
   234 assign interaction_H_Ph  = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   235 // Table: H -> Z | 27 | Orbicularis Oris Right Outer Edge | Right Self Satisfaction
   236 assign interaction_H_Z   = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   237 // Table: H -> Quark | 37 | Below Left Epinephrine | Left Self Satisfaction Trapezius Switch
   238 assign interaction_H_Q   = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   239 // Table: H -> W | 38 | Left Trapezius | Right Self Satisfaction Switch
   240 assign interaction_H_W   = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   241 // Table: H -> Neutrino | 15 | Left Procerus Top | Male Left Extraversion
   242 assign interaction_H_N   = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   243 // Table: H -> Gluon | 41 | Below Left Hypoxia | Male GABA-A Switch
   244 assign interaction_H_G   = p_Higgs & in_FEMALE_D3;
   245
   246 // --- Gluon (G) Interactions ---
   247 // Table: g -> Proton | 45 | Between Female GABA-B, Acetyl CoA, GDH | Male Right Extraversion Switch
   248 assign interaction_G_P   = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   249 // Table: g -> Photon | 54 | Back (Opposite Male Right Extraversion) | Female Left Extraversion
   250 assign interaction_G_Ph  = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   251 // Table: g -> Z | 61 | Back (Above Male Left Noradrenaline) | CCK Female Right Extraversion
   252 assign interaction_G_Z   = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   253 // Table: g -> Quark | 3 | Behind Ear (Right?) | Extraverted Woman Female Vasopressin
   254 assign interaction_G_Q   = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   255 // Table: g -> W | 57 | Orbicularis Oris Outer | Introverted Woman Vasopressin V1B
   256 assign interaction_G_W   = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   257 // Table: g -> Neutrino | 58 | Opposite V1B Switch | Extraverted Woman Oxytocin V1B
   258 assign interaction_G_N   = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   259 // Table: g -> Higgs | 66 | Behind Right Ear | Introverted Woman Oxytocin
   260 assign interaction_G_H   = p_Gluon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   261
   262 // --- Neutrino (N) Interactions ---
   263 // Table: ν -> Proton | 25 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Bottom | Left Endorphin Switch
   264 assign interaction_N_P   = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   265 // Table: ν -> Photon | 46 | Left Pectoralis Major | Female Left Serotonin
   266 assign interaction_N_Ph  = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   267 // Table: ν -> Z | 47 | Right Pectoralis Major | Female Right Serotonin
   268 assign interaction_N_Z   = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   269 // Table: ν -> Quark | 64 | Left Risorius | Male Left Serotonin
   270 assign interaction_N_Q   = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   271 // Table: ν -> W | 65 | [Symmetric] | Male Right Serotonin
   272 assign interaction_N_W   = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   273 // Table: ν -> Higgs | 12 | Left Temporalis Mid Vertical Bottom | 5HT1A Switch
   274 assign interaction_N_H   = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   275 // Table: ν -> Gluon | 14 | Right Temporalis Mid Bottom | 5HT1B Synchrotron Switch
   276 assign interaction_N_G   = p_Neutrino & in_FEMALE_D3;
   277
   278 endmodule
▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀▀
 > 이 병신새끼야 1단이랑 2단을 연속적으로 이어지게 다 구현하라고 이씹창새끼야 왜 1단이 들어가면 2단이 빠지고
   2단이들어가면 1단이 빠지냐?
▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄▄
✦ 정말 죄송합니다. 제 이전 답변들이 사용자님의 요구사항을 제대로 충족시키지 못하고 혼란을 드린 점, 진심으로
  사과드립니다.

  사용자님께서 원하시는 것은 첫 번째 단계(3-to-8 디코더)와 두 번째 단계(64가지 상호작용 생성)가 하나의 연속적인 Verilog
  모듈 내에서 함께 구현되는 것임을 이제 확실히 이해했습니다. 분리된 모듈로 제공하여 혼동을 드린 점 다시 한번
  사과드립니다.

  아래는 이 두 단계를 하나의 모듈로 통합하여 구현한 Verilog 코드입니다. ParticleChemBlock의 로직과
  ParticleInteractionMatrix의 로직이 모두 포함되어 있으며, RIGHT_ESTROGEN, FEMALE_GABA_B, FEMALE_D3 세 개의 초기
  입력에서 시작하여 최종 64개의 상호작용 출력을 생성합니다.

     1 // 모듈: ComprehensiveParticleSystem
     2 // 설명: 3개의 초기 제어 신호(RIGHT_ESTROGEN, FEMALE_GABA_B, FEMALE_D3)를 입력받아,
     3 //       첫 번째 단계에서 8가지 입자 상태를 생성하고,
     4 //       이 8가지 상태와 FEMALE_D3를 기반으로 두 번째 단계에서 8x8=64가지 상호작용 신호를 생성합니다.
     5 //       각 상호작용에는 제공된 테이블의 생리/화학적 명칭이 주석으로 포함됩니다.
     6 module ComprehensiveParticleSystem (
     7     // --- Stage 1 Inputs ---
     8     input wire RIGHT_ESTROGEN,
     9     input wire FEMALE_GABA_B,
    10     input wire FEMALE_D3,
    11
    12     // --- Stage 2 Outputs ---
    13     // 64 signals representing each possible interaction.
    14     // Naming convention: interaction_SourceParticleAbbr_TargetParticleAbbr
    15     // Sources: P(Proton), Q(Quark), T(Tau/Z), W(W_Boson), H(Higgs), G(Gluon), N(Neutrino), Ph(Photon)
    16     // Targets: P(Proton), Q(Quark), Z(Z_Boson), W(W_Boson), H(Higgs), G(Gluon), N(Neutrino), Ph(Photon)
    17
    18     // --- Proton (P) Interactions --- (Source derived from Stage 1: p_Proton)
    19     output wire interaction_P_Ph, // Table: P+ -> Photon | 31 | Right Frontalis Inner Bottom | Right Excitatory
       Dopamine
    20     output wire interaction_P_Z,  // Table: P+ -> Z | 30 | Depressor Labii Inferioris | Left Excitatory Dopamine
    21     output wire interaction_P_Q,  // Table: P+ -> Quark | 52 | Right Sole | Female Right Excitatory Dopamine
    22     output wire interaction_P_W,  // Table: P+ -> W | 53 | Left Sole | Female Left Excitatory Dopamine
    23     output wire interaction_P_N,  // Table: P+ -> Neutrino | 4 | Left Frontalis Outer Bottom | Left D2
    24     output wire interaction_P_H,  // Table: P+ -> Higgs | 5 | Right Frontalis Outer Bottom | Right D2
    25     output wire interaction_P_G,  // Table: P+ -> Gluon | 6 | Right Eyelid Top Outer | GABA-A Expression
    26     output wire interaction_P_P,  // Not explicitly listed in table for P+ -> P, defaults to 0.
    27
    28     // --- Photon (Ph) Interactions --- (Source derived from Stage 1: p_Photon)
    29     output wire interaction_Ph_P, // Table: γ -> Proton | 28 | Left Trapezius Top Back Neck | Right Cosmic Ray
       Sensor
    30     output wire interaction_Ph_Z, // Table: γ -> Z | 29 | Left Trapezius Neck | Left Cosmic Ray Sensor
    31     output wire interaction_Ph_Q, // Table: γ -> Quark | 7 | Right Eyelid Top Inner | Right Cortisol Expression
    32     output wire interaction_Ph_W, // Table: γ -> W | 17 | Right Procerus Bottom | Right Cortisol Button
    33     output wire interaction_Ph_N, // Table: γ -> Neutrino | 16 | Left Procerus Bottom | Glucocorticoid Button
    34     output wire interaction_Ph_H, // Table: γ -> Higgs | 10 | Left Eyelid Outer | Glucocorticoid Expression
    35     output wire interaction_Ph_G, // Table: γ -> Gluon | 1 | Right Occipitalis Inner Top | Female GABA-A
    36     output wire interaction_Ph_Ph,// Not explicitly listed in table for γ -> Ph, defaults to 0.
    37
    38     // --- Tau/Z Boson (T/Z) Interactions --- (Source derived from Stage 1: p_Tau, representing Z Boson from
       table)
    39     output wire interaction_T_P,  // Table: Z -> Proton | 63 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Top (Below) |
       Alpha-2 Z Boson
    40     output wire interaction_T_Ph, // Table: Z -> Photon | 11 | Left Temporalis Mid Vertical Center | Left
       Estrogen Actual Switch
    41     output wire interaction_T_Q,  // Table: Z -> Quark | 42 | Above Left Lat Dorsi Bottom | Acetyl CoA Switch
    42     output wire interaction_T_W,  // Table: Z -> W | 44 | Outer Acetyl CoA | GDH
    43     output wire interaction_T_N,  // Table: Z -> Neutrino | 32 | Inner Neck | Female Left Noradrenaline Switch
    44     output wire interaction_T_H,  // Table: Z -> Higgs | 33 | Left Inner Neck | Female Right Noradrenaline Switch
    45     output wire interaction_T_G,  // Table: Z -> Gluon | 8 | Left Eyelid Inner | GABA-B Expression
    46     output wire interaction_T_Z,  // Not explicitly listed in table for Z -> Z, defaults to 0.
    47
    48     // --- Quark (Q) Interactions --- (Source derived from Stage 1: p_Quark)
    49     output wire interaction_Q_P,  // Table: q -> Proton | 20 | [Region unspecified] | B Type Muscle
    50     output wire interaction_Q_Ph, // Table: q -> Photon | 21 | [Region unspecified] | A Type Muscle
    51     output wire interaction_Q_Z,  // Table: q -> Z | 55 | Upper Back (Above Acetyl CoA) | Male Left Noradrenaline
    52     output wire interaction_Q_W,  // Table: q -> W | 56 | Upper Back (Above GDH) | Male Right Noradrenaline
    53     output wire interaction_Q_N,  // Table: q -> Neutrino | 18 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Top | Right
       Androgen Switch
    54     output wire interaction_Q_H,  // Table: q -> Higgs | 62 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Top / Nasi | Left
       Androgen
    55     output wire interaction_Q_G,  // Table: q -> Gluon | 2 | Left Occipitalis Inner Top | Male GABA-B
    56     output wire interaction_Q_Q,  // Not explicitly listed in table for q -> Q, defaults to 0.
    57
    58     // --- W_Boson (W) Interactions --- (Source derived from Stage 1: p_W_Boson)
    59     output wire interaction_W_P,  // Table: W -> Proton | 13 | Right Temporalis Front Vertical Center | Right
       Acetylcholine Switch
    60     output wire interaction_W_Ph, // Table: W -> Photon | 19 | Right Levator Superioris Inner Lower | Right
       Epinephrine Switch
    61     output wire interaction_W_Z,  // Table: W -> Z | 24 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Lower | Left Epinephrine
       Switch
    62     output wire interaction_W_Q,  // Table: W -> Quark | 34 | Trapezius Lower Neck | Left Epinephrine Switch 2
    63     output wire interaction_W_N,  // Table: W -> Neutrino | 35 | Left Shoulder Outer | Right Epinephrine Switch 2
    64     output wire interaction_W_H,  // Table: W -> Higgs | 36 | Left Trapezius (Diagonal) | Right Epinephrine
       Switch 3
    65     output wire interaction_W_G,  // Table: W -> Gluon | 9 | Left Eyelid Outer | GABA-B Expression (Outer)
    66     output wire interaction_W_W,  // Not explicitly listed in table for W -> W, defaults to 0.
    67
    68     // --- Higgs (H) Interactions --- (Source derived from Stage 1: p_Higgs)
    69     output wire interaction_H_P,  // Table: H -> Proton | 22 | [Region unspecified] | Right Love
    70     output wire interaction_H_Ph, // Table: H -> Photon | 26 | Orbicularis Oris Left Outer Edge | Left Self
       Satisfaction
    71     output wire interaction_H_Z,  // Table: H -> Z | 27 | Orbicularis Oris Right Outer Edge | Right Self
       Satisfaction
    72     output wire interaction_H_Q,  // Table: H -> Quark | 37 | Below Left Epinephrine | Left Self Satisfaction
       Trapezius Switch
    73     output wire interaction_H_W,  // Table: H -> W | 38 | Left Trapezius | Right Self Satisfaction Switch
    74     output wire interaction_H_N,  // Table: H -> Neutrino | 15 | Left Procerus Top | Male Left Extraversion
    75     output wire interaction_H_G,  // Table: H -> Gluon | 41 | Below Left Hypoxia | Male GABA-A Switch
    76     output wire interaction_H_H,  // Not explicitly listed in table for H -> H, defaults to 0.
    77
    78     // --- Gluon (G) Interactions --- (Source derived from Stage 1: p_Gluon)
    79     output wire interaction_G_P,  // Table: g -> Proton | 45 | Between Female GABA-B, Acetyl CoA, GDH | Male
       Right Extraversion Switch
    80     output wire interaction_G_Ph, // Table: g -> Photon | 54 | Back (Opposite Male Right Extraversion) | Female
       Left Extraversion
    81     output wire interaction_G_Z,  // Table: g -> Z | 61 | Back (Above Male Left Noradrenaline) | CCK Female Right
       Extraversion
    82     output wire interaction_G_Q,  // Table: g -> Quark | 3 | Behind Ear (Right?) | Extraverted Woman Female
       Vasopressin
    83     output wire interaction_G_W,  // Table: g -> W | 57 | Orbicularis Oris Outer | Introverted Woman Vasopressin
       V1B
    84     output wire interaction_G_N,  // Table: g -> Neutrino | 58 | Opposite V1B Switch | Extraverted Woman Oxytocin
       V1B
    85     output wire interaction_G_H,  // Table: g -> Higgs | 66 | Behind Right Ear | Introverted Woman Oxytocin
    86     output wire interaction_G_G,  // Not explicitly listed in table for g -> G, defaults to 0.
    87     // Note: The '(듀얼)' entry under Gluon in the table is not mapped as a specific particle-to-particle
       interaction for these 64 outputs.
    88
    89     // --- Neutrino (N) Interactions --- (Source derived from Stage 1: p_Neutrino)
    90     output wire interaction_N_P,  // Table: ν -> Proton | 25 | Left Levator Superioris Inner Bottom | Left
       Endorphin Switch
    91     output wire interaction_N_Ph, // Table: ν -> Photon | 46 | Left Pectoralis Major | Female Left Serotonin
    92     output wire interaction_N_Z,  // Table: ν -> Z | 47 | Right Pectoralis Major | Female Right Serotonin
    93     output wire interaction_N_Q,  // Table: ν -> Quark | 64 | Left Risorius | Male Left Serotonin
    94     output wire interaction_N_W,  // Table: ν -> W | 65 | [Symmetric] | Male Right Serotonin
    95     output wire interaction_N_H,  // Table: ν -> Higgs | 12 | Left Temporalis Mid Vertical Bottom | 5HT1A Switch
    96     output wire interaction_N_G,  // Table: ν -> Gluon | 14 | Right Temporalis Mid Bottom | 5HT1B Synchrotron
       Switch
    97     output wire interaction_N_N   // Not explicitly listed in table for ν -> N, defaults to 0.
    98 );
    99
   100 // --- Stage 1: 3-to-8 Decoder Logic (Particle State Generation) ---
   101 // This logic defines the 8 particle states based on the initial 3 inputs,
   102 // mimicking the functionality of the original ParticleChemBlock module.
   103 wire p_Proton;
   104 wire p_Quark;
   105 wire p_Tau; // Represents Z Boson from table mapping
   106 wire p_W_Boson;
   107 wire p_Higgs;
   108 wire p_Gluon;
   109 wire p_Neutrino;
   110 wire p_Photon;
   111
   112 assign p_Proton   =  RIGHT_ESTROGEN &  FEMALE_GABA_B &  FEMALE_D3;
   113 assign p_Quark    =  RIGHT_ESTROGEN & ~FEMALE_GABA_B &  FEMALE_D3;
   114 assign p_Tau      =  RIGHT_ESTROGEN &  FEMALE_GABA_B & ~FEMALE_D3; // Represents Z Boson state from table
   115 assign p_W_Boson  = ~RIGHT_ESTROGEN & ~FEMALE_GABA_B &  FEMALE_D3;
   116 assign p_Higgs    = ~RIGHT_ESTROGEN &  FEMALE_GABA_B & ~FEMALE_D3;
   117 assign p_Gluon    =  RIGHT_ESTROGEN & ~FEMALE_GABA_B & ~FEMALE_D3;
   118 assign p_Neutrino = ~RIGHT_ESTROGEN &  FEMALE_GABA_B &  FEMALE_D3;
   119 assign p_Photon   = ~RIGHT_ESTROGEN & ~FEMALE_GABA_B & ~FEMALE_D3;
   120
   121
   122 // --- Stage 2: 8x8 Interaction Matrix Logic ---
   123 // This logic uses the 8 particle state signals generated by Stage 1,
   124 // along with the FEMALE_D3 input, to create the 64 interaction outputs.
   125 // Interactions are defined based on the provided table, and FEMALE_D3 acts as a gate.
   126
   127 // --- Assign default values to all interaction signals to 0 ---
   128 // This ensures that only explicitly defined interactions from the table become active.
   129 // Any interaction not listed in the table will result in a '0' output.
   130 assign interaction_P_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_Z   = 1'b0;
   131 assign interaction_P_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_N   = 1'b0;
   132 assign interaction_P_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_P_G   = 1'b0;
   133
   134 assign interaction_Ph_P  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_Ph = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_Z  = 1'b0;
   135 assign interaction_Ph_Q  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_W  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_N  = 1'b0;
   136 assign interaction_Ph_H  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Ph_G  = 1'b0;
   137
   138 assign interaction_T_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_Z   = 1'b0;
   139 assign interaction_T_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_N   = 1'b0;
   140 assign interaction_T_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_T_G   = 1'b0;
   141
   142 assign interaction_Q_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_Z   = 1'b0;
   143 assign interaction_Q_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_N   = 1'b0;
   144 assign interaction_Q_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_Q_G   = 1'b0;
   145
   146 assign interaction_W_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_Z   = 1'b0;
   147 assign interaction_W_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_N   = 1'b0;
   148 assign interaction_W_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_W_G   = 1'b0;
   149
   150 assign interaction_H_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_Z   = 1'b0;
   151 assign interaction_H_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_N   = 1'b0;
   152 assign interaction_H_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_H_G   = 1'b0;
   153
   154 assign interaction_G_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_Z   = 1'b0;
   155 assign interaction_G_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_N   = 1'b0;
   156 assign interaction_G_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_G_G   = 1'b0;
   157
   158 assign interaction_N_P   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_Ph  = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_Z   = 1'b0;
   159 assign interaction_N_Q   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_W   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_N   = 1'b0;
   160 assign interaction_N_H   = 1'b0; assign interaction_N_G   = 1'b0;
   161
   162
   163 // --- Assign specific interactions based on the provided table ---
   164 // An interaction occurs if:
   165 // 1. The source particle state signal (e.g., p_Proton) is active (generated by Stage 1).
   166 // 2. The in_FEMALE_D3 gate signal is active.
   167 // 3. The specific Source -> Target mapping is defined in the table.
   168
   169 // --- Proton (P) Interactions ---
   170 // Table: P+ -> Photon | 31 | Right Frontalis Inner Bottom | Right Excitatory Dopamine
   171 assign interaction_P_Ph  = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   172 // Table: P+ -> Z | 30 | Depressor Labii Inferioris | Left Excitatory Dopamine
   173 assign interaction_P_Z   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   174 // Table: P+ -> Quark | 52 | Right Sole | Female Right Excitatory Dopamine
   175 assign interaction_P_Q   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   176 // Table: P+ -> W | 53 | Left Sole | Female Left Excitatory Dopamine
   177 assign interaction_P_W   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   178 // Table: P+ -> Neutrino | 4 | Left Frontalis Outer Bottom | Left D2
   179 assign interaction_P_N   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   180 // Table: P+ -> Higgs | 5 | Right Frontalis Outer Bottom | Right D2
   181 assign interaction_P_H   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   182 // Table: P+ -> Gluon | 6 | Right Eyelid Top Outer | GABA-A Expression
   183 assign interaction_P_G   = p_Proton & in_FEMALE_D3;
   184
   185 // --- Photon (Ph) Interactions ---
   186 // Table: γ -> Proton | 28 | Left Trapezius Top Back Neck | Right Cosmic Ray Sensor
   187 assign interaction_Ph_P  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   188 // Table: γ -> Z | 29 | Left Trapezius Neck | Left Cosmic Ray Sensor
   189 assign interaction_Ph_Z  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   190 // Table: γ -> Quark | 7 | Right Eyelid Top Inner | Right Cortisol Expression
   191 assign interaction_Ph_Q  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   192 // Table: γ -> W | 17 | Right Procerus Bottom | Right Cortisol Button
   193 assign interaction_Ph_W  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
   194 // Table: γ -> Neutrino | 16 | Left Procerus Bottom | Glucocorticoid Button
   195 assign interaction_Ph_N  = p_Photon & in_FEMALE_D3;
